# Meet The Doctors

## Dr. O'Gara, Dr. Gilbert, and Dr. Silvaroli are improving the lives of children and adults, one smile at a time

Meet The Doctors[Unravel](https://ogortho.com/author/starburst-pink138/)2026-04-13T19:24:21+00:00

![](https://ogortho.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/OG2025-179_compressed.jpg "OG2025-179_compressed")

## Voted Reno’s Best Orthodontist 2021, 2022, & 2023!

### See the difference at O’Gara-Gilbert-Silvaroli Orthodontics

Dr. Gilbert, Dr. O’Gara, & Dr. Silvaroli are outstanding and caring orthodontists committed to helping patients achieve their dream smile.

[Schedule Your Visit](/contact/)

![Photo of Dr. O'Gara, Dr. Gilbert, and Dr. Silvaroli](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%27http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%27%20width%3D%271500%27%20height%3D%27897%27%20viewBox%3D%270%200%201500%20897%27%3E%3Crect%20width%3D%271500%27%20height%3D%27897%27%20fill-opacity%3D%220%22%2F%3E%3C%2Fsvg%3E "OGJan23-69 (1)")

## Dr. William P. O’Gara

![](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%27http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%27%20width%3D%27666%27%20height%3D%27895%27%20viewBox%3D%270%200%20666%20895%27%3E%3Crect%20width%3D%27666%27%20height%3D%27895%27%20fill-opacity%3D%220%22%2F%3E%3C%2Fsvg%3E "OGJan23-156")

Dr. William P. O’Gara (DDS) was born and raised in Reno, Nevada. He is a long-time native Nevadan esteemed for his outstanding orthodontic treatment results and clinical expertise. Dr. O’Gara graduated from Bishop Manogue Catholic High School and went on to pursue his Bachelor of Science in Biology from the University of Nevada.  After graduating Summa Cum Laude, Dr. O’Gara was accepted into the top nationally-ranked Arthur A. Dugoni School of Dentistry, at the University of Pacific, San Francisco. While in dental school, Dr. O’Gara was awarded the Gold Foil Award for Clinical Excellence and graduated top of his class. After graduating from Dental School, Dr. O’Gara practiced as a general dentist for two years before he made the decision to further his passion for teeth by attending the University of Louisville orthodontic residency program. Dr. O’Gara had the honor of returning as a distinguished alumni lecturer.

Shortly after residency, Dr. O’Gara founded O’Gara Orthodontics in 1990 and has since been an active participant in the Dental and Orthodontic community.

Dr. O’Gara is committed to pursuing excellence and has accomplished the following:

-   Delegate for the Young Dentists of America
-   Hands on Herbst appliance training
-   Appointed Delegate for Nevada Dental Association
-   Appointed by Governor Sandoval to the Nevada State Board of Dental Examiners
-   President of Northern Nevada Dental Association
-   Recipient of the RGJ Best Orthodontist in Reno 2021
-   McLaughlin straight wire training
-   Dentaurum temporary anchorage device training
-   Graduate of Fort Knox Advanced Orthodontic Education Program
-   Member of National Dental Honor Society, Omicron Kappa Upsilon
-   Member of the American Dental Association
-   Member of the Pacific Coast Society of Orthodontics
-   Member of the American Association of Orthodontists
-   Member of the Nevada Dental Association
-   Member of the Northern Nevada Dental Association

In February of 2021, Dr. O’Gara was proud to welcome Dr. K.C. Gilbert as a partner – forming O’Gara-Gilbert Orthodontics. Dr. O’Gara is honored to partner with Dr. Gilbert because each share a similar vision for orthodontic excellence, focusing on delivering the highest orthodontic experience and treatment. Achieving beautiful and healthy smiles is the foundation of our practice philosophy!

Dr. O’Gara is passionate about his career and feels privileged to serve his community. He is enthusiastic about the interactions and relationships between his patients and parents.  He and his wife Valerie have 2 children, and they are proud to call Reno their home. When Dr. O’Gara is not crafting smiles, you can find him, golfing, cooking, boating, traveling, and enjoying life with his family and friends!

![](https://ogortho.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/03/OGJan23-156-e1709589289735.jpg "OGJan23-156")

## Dr. K.C. Gilbert

![](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%27http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%27%20width%3D%271706%27%20height%3D%272291%27%20viewBox%3D%270%200%201706%202291%27%3E%3Crect%20width%3D%271706%27%20height%3D%272291%27%20fill-opacity%3D%220%22%2F%3E%3C%2Fsvg%3E "OGJan23-201")

K.C. Gilbert, DMD, MS is a Nevada native born and raised here in Reno. He graduated from Bishop Manogue High School and went on to receive his degree in Biochemistry and Molecular Biology from the University of Nevada, Reno.

After finishing his undergraduate degree and discovering a passion for dentistry, Dr. Gilbert was accepted into dental school at Oregon Health and Science University. While in dental school Dr. Gilbert served as the OHSU Student Body President, OHSU School of Dentistry Vice President, and the president of his dental school class. He also worked hard to finish in the top 5% of his class and earned numerous accolades including…

-   Induction into the National Dental Honor Society, Omicron Kappa Upsilon
-   Recipient of the faculty nominated Pierre Fauchard Foundation Dental Student Scholarship Award
-   Recipient of the MODA/Oregon Dental Society Scholarship
-   Recipient of the OHSU Dental School Leadership award which is awarded to one dental graduate every year who has shown and shows great future potential in leadership
-   Recipient of the OHSU Dental School Biology Award which is awarded to one dental graduate every year who has made a significant contribution in the science of oral biology
-   Recipient of the OCTRI Sumer Fellowship Award for Research
-   Primary author of a published textbook chapter; In: Epidemiology – Theory, Research and Practice
-   Co-primary author of research presented at the Oregon Dental Conference as well as the prestigious annual International Association of Dental Research general session

After dental school, Dr. Gilbert went on to train at the competitive University of Florida Orthodontic residency program where he earned a Certificate in Orthodontics and a Master of Science in Dental Sciences with specialization in orthodontics. During his third and final year of residency, Dr. Gilbert was nominated and served as Chief Resident of the program.

Once Dr. Gilbert completed his training he and his wife were thrilled to move back to his hometown and serve the community that he grew up loving. He is incredibly passionate for his career and believes it’s a great privilege to provide children, teens, and adults with beautiful smiles that they can be proud of. The opportunity to join Dr. Bill O’Gara and his team was a dream come true for Dr. Gilbert and he is committed to provide the same excellent care and attention to detail that Dr. O’Gara has given the Reno community throughout his career. He is now actively involved in organized dentistry in Nevada, holding positions in both the Nevada Dental Association as well as the Northern Nevada Dental Association. In his free time, Dr. Gilbert and his wife, Erika, love taking advantage of all the great outdoor activities that Northern Nevada has to offer. They are avid sports fans and greatly enjoy watching almost any sporting event they can together with family and friends.

![](https://ogortho.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/03/OGJan23-201-scaled-e1709589308887.jpg "OGJan23-201")

## Dr. John Silvaroli

![](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%27http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%27%20width%3D%271706%27%20height%3D%272120%27%20viewBox%3D%270%200%201706%202120%27%3E%3Crect%20width%3D%271706%27%20height%3D%272120%27%20fill-opacity%3D%220%22%2F%3E%3C%2Fsvg%3E "OGJan23-161")

Dr. John Silvaroli has called Reno home since 1997. He is a Galena High School graduate and completed his undergraduate work at the University of Nevada, Reno.

Dr. Silvaroli then pursued his dental education at UNLV School of Dental Medicine, graduating Magna Cum Laude and at the top of his class. During his time at UNLV SDM, he was the president of the SGT Ferrin Veteran’s Memorial Clinic and conducted oral cancer research. He was honored to present his research as the keynote oral presenter for Nutritional Sciences at the International Association of Dental Research in Seoul, Korea.

Numerous other achievements for Dr. Silvaroli’s include:

-   Authored two journal publications
-   Inducted into the National Dental Honor Society, Omicron Kappa Upsilon
-   Awarded the OKU William S. Kramer Award of Excellence
-   Recipient of the award for the Development of the Oro-Facial Complex by the American Association of Orthodontists (AAO)

Following dental school, John had the incredible opportunity to practice general dentistry alongside his brother, Dr. Patrick Silvaroli. With the support of his brother, John pursued further specialization in orthodontics at the University of Oklahoma. The OU graduate orthodontics program exposed him to many facets of orthodontics and dentofacial orthopedics including early treatment in the primary dentition, cleft and craniofacial treatment and management, and multidisciplinary treatment involving oral surgery, periodontics, and restorative dentistry. He defended his thesis titled, _The Incidence of Parafunctional Habits in Association with Clear Aligner Therapy,_ and received his Masters of Science.

Dr. John Silvaroli is very excited to return to the Northern Nevada community and treat patients in the area where he grew up. He is looking forward to staying involved in organized dentistry and taking opportunities to give back to the community. In his free time, John enjoys outdoor activities including snowboarding, golf, and taking advantage of all that Lake Tahoe has to offer. He cherishes moments with his family, friends, and Bernedoodle pup, Louie.

![](https://ogortho.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/03/OGJan23-161-scaled-e1709588865900.jpg "OGJan23-161")

![](https://ogortho.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/mission-statement-pop-up-3.png "mission statement pop up (3)")

# Schedule Your New Patient Exam

## No referral needed. No obligation. Just smiles.

New Patient Exam Scheduling[Unravel](https://ogortho.com/author/starburst-pink138/)2026-05-11T21:09:48+00:00

## We’re so excited to meet you!

Taking the first step toward your dream smile is easy — simply choose the option below that best describes you and pick a time that works for your schedule. Your complimentary exam includes a full evaluation, digital imaging if needed, and a conversation with one of our doctors about your smile goals and treatment options.

## Child/Teen

Whether it’s an early evaluation for your little one or time to get started on braces or aligners for your teen, we make the first visit fun, comfortable, and stress-free for the whole family.

[Schedule My Child/Teen Exam](https://edgebooking.ortho2.com/search?catalog=e3846f4b-7093-4f07-8c81-f08856c6c4d6&code=znxfkn "Schedule My Adult New Patient Exam")

## Adult

Ready to invest in yourself? It’s never too late for a beautiful, confident smile. Whether you’re curious about clear aligners, traditional braces, or something in between, we’d love to walk you through your options.

[Schedule My Adult Exam](https://edgebooking.ortho2.com/search?catalog=e3846f4b-7093-4f07-8c81-f08856c6c4d6&code=ntvmwu "Schedule My Adult New Patient Exam")

## Two Family Members

Scheduling for two? We’d love to welcome multiple family members at once! Whether that’s siblings, a parent and child, or any combination. Be sure to mention that you’re booking for two when you schedule. 

[Schedule My Two Family Members Exam](https://edgebooking.ortho2.com/search?catalog=e3846f4b-7093-4f07-8c81-f08856c6c4d6&code=ntvmwu "Schedule My Adult New Patient Exam")

## Thank you for voting O’Gara-Gilbert-Silvaroli Orthodontics as Best Orthodontist in 2026!

We’re thrilled to announce that O’Gara-Gilbert-Silvaroli Orthodontics has been named Best Orthodontist in the 2026 Best of Reno Community’s Choice Awards! This incredible honor is a testament to the trust and support of our amazing patients and the Reno community who voted to recognize the exceptional care we strive to deliver every day.

The Best of Reno Awards celebrate the top businesses and organizations across more than 180 categories, and being selected as a winner is a true community-driven achievement. Thank you for making us your favorite. We’re proud to serve you and honored to be part of what makes Reno shine!

# Our Culture

## Excellence, Compassion, and Community are at the heart of our practice

Our Culture[Unravel](https://ogortho.com/author/starburst-pink138/)2026-06-03T17:11:50+00:00

### Thank You for Voting O’Gara-Gilbert-Silvaroli Orthodontics as Best Orthodontist in 2026!

We’re thrilled to announce that O’Gara-Gilbert-Silvaroli Orthodontics has been named Best Orthodontist in the 2025 Best of Reno Community’s Choice Awards! This incredible honor is a testament to the trust and support of our amazing patients and the Reno community, who voted to recognize the exceptional care we strive to deliver every day.

The Best of Reno Awards celebrate the top businesses and organizations across more than 180 categories, and being selected as a winner is a true community-driven achievement. Thank you for making us your favorite. We’re proud to serve you and honored to be part of what makes Reno shine!

-   ![](https://ogortho.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/800-x-534.jpg)
-   ![](https://ogortho.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/docs-rgj-e1780428749185.jpg)
-   ![Best of Northern Nevada 2024 award that O'Gara-Gilbert-Silvaroli Orthodontics received](https://ogortho.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/10/Dr.-O-RNR-300x200.jpg)
-   ![Dr. Gilbert, Dr. O'Gara, and Dr. Silvaroli group photo](https://ogortho.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/04/0cc97c36-1f0b-ac3a-d22a-3003d3ae7a2f-300x200.jpg)
-   ![Dr. O'Gara and Dr. Gilbert and the Best of Reno awards event](https://ogortho.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/04/68ad9f6f-a759-f0a6-cc7b-46982b18001a-300x200.jpg)
-   ![Dr. O'Gara and Dr. Gilbert with friends and family at the Best of Reno awards event](https://ogortho.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/04/000f9091-f2cf-3456-71c4-609ca4268fc8-300x200.jpg)

-   ![OG Ortho staff baseball team photo](https://ogortho.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/04/886f7836-4399-c531-206d-62c5e03a203b-300x200.jpg)
-   ![Team celebration with the doctors opening champagne outside](https://ogortho.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/05/DSC07357-300x200.jpg)
-   ![5 staff members dressed as clowns in the practice's brand colors](https://ogortho.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/05/IMG_0823-300x200.jpg)

### A Culture of Compassion and Fun

O’Gara-Gilbert-Silvaroli Orthodontics is proud of our vibrant team culture, characterized by compassion, teamwork, and a dash of fun. Our practice is not just about perfecting smiles; it’s about creating a welcoming atmosphere where patients and staff feel valued.

We believe in the power of teamwork and joy in the workplace and celebrate our team’s achievements with a monthly Difference Maker Award. We also enjoy hosting events like Friendsgiving feasts, team-building activities, escape rooms in festive pajamas, and celebrating holidays with fun competitions.

### Community Engagement

O’Gara-Gilbert-Silvaroli Orthodontics is deeply rooted in the Reno community. We believe in giving back through initiatives that foster health, wellness, and joy. From organizing successful events like the Healthy Smiles Raffle and the Candy Trade Out—which saw over 210 lbs of candy donated to the Veterans Guest House—to engaging our patients and local residents with fun giveaways and contests, we strive to make a positive impact. Our practice is not only about transforming smiles, but also about creating meaningful connections and contributing to the wellbeing of our Northern Nevada community.

![Dr. Gilbert giving a presentation on braces at a community event](https://ogortho.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/04/d6447beb-e85e-de15-9994-64f6651433bf.jpg "d6447beb-e85e-de15-9994-64f6651433bf")

## Why Choose O’Gara-Gilbert-Silvaroli Orthodontics

Experience modern orthodontic care from Reno’s leading orthodontists, now serving Fernley. Every smile starts with personalized attention and advanced technology designed to deliver results you’ll love.

### What Sets Us Apart

-   **Three Experts, One Price:** Get the combined expertise of Dr. O’Gara, Dr. Gilbert, and Dr. Silvaroli for the cost of one.

### Common Questions from Our Patients

#### [What orthodontic treatments do you offer in Fernley?](#be082e3b26b5e8aa3)

Our Fernley location offers the same comprehensive orthodontic treatments as our Reno office, including traditional braces, clear aligners, retainers, and early orthodontic care for children. We also provide treatment for teens and adults using advanced digital technology for precise, comfortable results.

#### [How do I know if I need braces or clear aligners?](#2c74bce43dffc751f)

During your complimentary consultation, our board-certified orthodontists will assess your bite and smile to recommend the best option.

#### [Do you treat children, teens, and adults in Fernley?](#cc0c7197c0d28fcf5)

Yes! We proudly offer orthodontic treatment for all ages. Whether your child needs an early orthodontic evaluation or you’re an adult looking to improve your smile, our Fernley team provides personalized care every step of the way.

#### [How much do braces or clear aligners cost in Fernley?](#f26d2eedfe7447f73)

The cost of orthodontic treatment varies depending on your individual case. We offer flexible payment plans and accept most dental insurance to make high-quality orthodontic care affordable for families in Fernley and nearby communities.

#### [How long does orthodontic treatment take?](#8e72b3836ad559ca3)

Every smile is unique, so treatment time varies for each patient. After your initial consultation, our doctors will provide a personalized timeline based on your orthodontic needs and goals.

#### [Where is your orthodontic office located in Fernley?](#dfa5d01371b15c32d)

We’re conveniently located at 1042 Inglewood Dr., Building G, Suite 100, Fernley, NV 89408, just off Highway 95A. Our office is easily accessible for patients from Fernley, Fallon, Silver Springs, and surrounding areas.

#### [How can I schedule an orthodontic appointment in Fernley?](#ab29029ca2b36e627)

You can schedule your visit by calling (775) 825-1881 or requesting an appointment online. Our friendly team will help you find a time that fits your schedule for your initial consultation.

## Why Choose O’Gara-Gilbert-Silvaroli Orthodontics

![](data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%27http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%27%20width%3D%271706%27%20height%3D%272120%27%20viewBox%3D%270%200%201706%202120%27%3E%3Crect%20width%3D%271706%27%20height%3D%272120%27%20fill-opacity%3D%220%22%2F%3E%3C%2Fsvg%3E "OGJan23-161")

Experience modern orthodontic care that puts you first. Every smile starts with personalized attention and advanced treatment technology designed to deliver results you’ll love.

### What Sets Us Apart

-   **Board-Certified Doctors:** Your care is always directed by our orthodontic specialists.  
    
-   **Advanced Technology:** Digital scanning and precision planning for faster, more comfortable results.  
    
-   **Convenient Locations:** Reno and Fernley offices with flexible scheduling.  
    
-   **Simple Financing:** Affordable payment plans and insurance support.
-   **Consistent Care:** The same trusted team at every appointment.

![](https://ogortho.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/12/OG2025-1-scaled.jpg "OG2025-1")

### Common Questions from Our Patients

#### [What ages do you treat?](#95ac0290f0f463d46)

We provide orthodontic care for children, teens, and adults. It’s never too early or too late to improve your smile.

#### [Do you accept insurance?](#ff91535f7eb4a5b7d)

The purpose of early evaluation is to inform and observe, more than to start treatment. At this early age, the eventual needs may be identified and discussed. A small percentage of this young group may need some form of intervention. Habits are evaluated, medical issues are discussed and an observation schedule is arranged until the patient is ready to begin treatment.

#### [How long will the first visit take?](#3f7371a60b38b9e87)

The first visit can last anywhere between 30 minutes and 1 hour depending on how ready the patient is for treatment. We may just do a visual exam and explain the situation or we may suggest one or more X-rays and complete diagnostics including impressions of the teeth with photographs to document the current position.

#### [Do you see patients for emergencies?](#81177103bee49323b)

Yes, our [contact information](/contact/) for an emergency is posted on the website. Feel free to visit our emergency care page for more information.

#### [Can we make payments?](#d226b4ba9d3e1aae1)

Yes. Whether or not you have insurance or a dental plan, we will structure a convenient payment plan that will allow you to pay for orthodontic services over a period of time that usually coincides with your treatment plan.

# All About Braces

## Guidelines for our patients

All About Braces[Unravel](https://ogortho.com/author/starburst-pink138/)2022-07-21T21:12:48+00:00

### General Soreness

When you get your braces on, you may feel general soreness in your mouth, and teeth may be tender to biting pressures for 3 – 5 days. If discomfort occurs, take over-the-counter pain medication as directed.

### Brushing and Flossing

It’s important to brush and floss regularly when you have braces so your teeth and gums are healthy during orthodontic treatment. We recommend professional dental cleanings every 3-6 months. Adults who have a history of gum disease should also see a periodontist during orthodontic treatment.

### Loose Wire or Band

If a wire sticks out and is irritating:

-   Use the eraser end of a pencil to gently push back the irritating end of the wire
-   Place wax or a wet cotton ball over the broken wire

Call our office as soon as possible for an appointment to check and repair.

### Rubber Band Wear

To successfully complete orthodontic treatment, the patient must work together with the orthodontist. The teeth and jaws can only move toward their corrected positions if the patient consistently wears the rubber bands or other appliances as prescribed.

### Athletics

A protective mouthguard is provided for playing contact sports.

### Eating with Braces

What can you eat? Let’s talk about what you shouldn’t eat! For the first day or so, stick to soft foods. Avoid tough meats, hard bread, and raw vegetables. Before long, you’ll be able to bit an apple again. But you’ll need to protect your orthodontic appliances when you eat for as long as you’re wearing braces.

**Foods to Avoid**

-   Chewy foods: bagels, hard rolls, licorice
-   Crunchy foods: popcorn, ice
-   Sticky foods: caramels, gum
-   Hard foods: nuts, candy
-   Foods you have to bit into such as corn on the cob, apples, carrots

Chewing on hard things (for example pens, pencils, or fingernails) can damage the braces. Damaged braces will cause treatment to take longer.

With a highly trained, professional staff

# Frequently Asked Questions

## Answering the most common questions regarding your orthodontic treatment in Reno, NV

Frequently Asked Questions[Unravel](https://ogortho.com/author/starburst-pink138/)2022-07-21T21:30:10+00:00

### Common Questions from Our Patients

#### [At what age do you start seeing patients?](#ded6d288cf43a7b80)

As a rule of thumb, we like to evaluate young patients after the 6-year molars erupt and the front permanent teeth just start to appear. This is usually between the ages of 6 and 8. The American Association of Orthodontics recommends that all children be seen for the first time by age 7.

#### [Why do you evaluate patients so young?](#b663cf3090bf38b7d)

The purpose of early evaluation is to inform and observe, more than to start treatment. At this early age, the eventual needs may be identified and discussed. A small percentage of this young group may need some form of intervention. Habits are evaluated, medical issues are discussed and an observation schedule is arranged until the patient is ready to begin treatment.

#### [How long will the first visit take?](#71dfe228b87038d10)

The first visit can last anywhere between 30 minutes and 1 hour depending on how ready the patient is for treatment. We may just do a visual exam and explain the situation or we may suggest one or more X-rays and complete diagnostics including impressions of the teeth with photographs to document the current position.

#### [Do you have adult patients?](#c9a7b1d06eb41366f)

Absolutely. A growing percentage of our patients are adults. New, more cosmetic appliances are making adult treatment much more comfortable and convenient.

#### [What is the difference between an orthodontist and a dentist?](#24a76fdd0e71aaab0)

An orthodontist is a general dentist that has had at least 2 more years of full-time training in orthodontics resulting in a nationally accepted specialty certificate. The orthodontist then limits their practice to straightening teeth and dentofacial orthopedics.

#### [Can I make all of my appointments in the afternoon, after school, and late in the day?](#629998a0885b320d1)

In order to have early and late appointments available for the majority of patient appointments, some procedures need to be accomplished during the hours of 9 and 3. We will do our best to work with your schedules and accommodate your family’s needs.

#### [Do you see patients for emergencies?](#f55d17fed2e19c471)

Yes, our [contact information](/contact/) for an emergency is posted on the website. Feel free to visit our emergency care page for more information.

#### [Can we make payments?](#c4197f755908d73f0)

Yes. Whether or not you have insurance or a dental plan, we will structure a convenient payment plan that will allow you to pay for orthodontic services over a period of time that usually coincides with your treatment plan.

#### [How do you handle insurance?](#af05966c61dc7072b)

After verification of coverage, we will agree to bill and collect from an insurance carrier. However, we ask patients to understand that if coverage ceases or changes, the patient is still responsible for the agreed-upon fee.

#### [Do you take American Express, Mastercard, or Visa?](#86786d65ad9688de4)

Yes, we accept all major credit cards.

#### [Do you have direct debit?](#0c88dfab5624abe74)

Yes, we have arrangements for direct debit, charge card posting, as well as payments over the internet.

#### [Do you charge interest?](#08ae64fcfc03af383)

No, we do not charge interest.

#### [Do you offer family discounts?](#375f3d8c25d30be9d)

We have a special program and fees for multiple patients from the same family.

#### [Do retainers need to be worn after braces?](#53d26e06af155c158)

Yes, retainers are worn full-time for about 6 months after treatment followed by nighttime wear indefinitely.

#### [Why do teeth get crooked after wearing braces or aligners?](#a57036874e085c07b)

Teeth move throughout life whether you have worn braces or not. It is part of the natural aging process. This is why retainer wear is recommended indefinitely to maintain your orthodontic results over a lifetime.

With a highly trained, professional staff

# Types of Braces

## We have orthodontic solutions to fit your lifestyle

Types of Braces[Unravel](https://ogortho.com/author/starburst-pink138/)2024-02-29T21:39:45+00:00

Thanks to today’s advances in orthodontic technology, O’Gara-Gilbert-Silvaroli Orthodontics can provide our patients with more options when it comes to wearing braces than ever before. While traditional metal braces are still widely used, clear braces are very popular with teens and adults who are self-conscious about wearing braces, along with Invisalign removable aligners, the “braceless” alternative to straight teeth. Dr. O’Gara, Dr. Gilbert, and Dr. Silvaroli will help you determine which treatment option is right for you.

### Metal Braces

Self-ligating metal braces are the most common type of braces used today.  They are smaller and more comfortable than ever before and constructed of high-grade space-age metal alloys. With metal braces, you have the option of adding colored elastics (rubber bands) for a more unique and colorful smile.

### Ceramic Braces

Ceramic braces are made of clear materials and are therefore less visible on your teeth than metal braces. For this reason, ceramic braces are used mainly on teenage and adult patients who have aesthetic concerns. While they are visually less prominent, they do require more attention to oral hygiene since ceramic braces are larger. The most common drawback to ceramic brackets is that they are more fragile and the elastic ties can discolor between orthodontic visits with certain foods and smoking.

![](https://ogortho.com/wp-content/uploads/2022/06/orthodontics.jpeg "beautiful laughing african american girl with dental braces, isolated on grey")

### Clear Aligners

Clear appliances, such as Invisalign, use a series of translucent, removable, and comfortable aligners to straighten your teeth. Invisalign aligners are removable so you are not limited in what you can eat or drink during treatment, making brushing and flossing less of a hassle. The aligners have no metal to cause mouth abrasions during treatment.

With a highly trained, professional staff

# Clear Aligners

## We're the clear aligner specialists in Reno, NV!

Clear Aligners[Unravel](https://ogortho.com/author/starburst-pink138/)2025-05-21T17:47:30+00:00

### Clear Aligners for Adults and Teens

Clear aligners are nearly invisible, allowing you to straighten your teeth discreetly. At OGS Ortho, we offer a range of clear aligner options—including Spark, Invisalign, and custom in-house aligners using our ULab system—to best suit your needs. Dr. O’Gara, Dr. Gilbert & Dr. Silvaroli are expert clear aligner treatment providers. They have treated many cases with this cutting-edge appliance system.

### Clear Aligners – “Braceless” Alternatives to Traditional Metal Braces!

Have you ever wished for a straighter, healthier smile without all the metal wires and bands that often go along with braces? At O’Gara-Gilbert-Silvaroli Orthodontics we can make your dream of having a new smile come to life. To learn more about clear aligners and to find out if you or your child is a candidate for clear aligners, contact our office today to schedule your clear aligners evaluation.

![](https://ogortho.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/07/young-smiling-woman-holding-invisalign-braces-over-2021-06-09-17-34-32-utc-1.jpg "young-smiling-woman-holding-invisalign-braces-over-2021-06-09-17-34-32-utc (1)")

### What are Clear Aligners?

Clear aligners use a series of custom-made trays to straighten teeth more quickly and with fewer office visits than traditional braces. Thousands of orthodontists in the United States and Canada use clear aligners to accurately and effectively treat their patients. Benefits of clear aligners are:

-   **Almost undetectable** – Clear aligners can barely be seen while you are wearing them! Friends, family members, and co-workers may _never even know you are wearing aligners unless of course, you tell them_!
-   **Comfortable to wear** – The aligners are made from a special plastic that has smooth, rounded edges so they _won’t irritate your mouth and gum tissue_.
-   **Customized just for you** – Your clear aligners are _customized to fit your mouth_. We use advanced 3-D computer imaging technology to replicate an exact impression of your mouth and teeth. Customized aligners are fabricated to fit your mouth, and over time move your teeth into the proper alignment.
-   **Removable** – You can remove your aligners to eat, drink, brush, floss, and for special occasions. _There’s no need to change your lifestyle with clear aligners_!
-   **Effective** – You will begin to notice a significant difference even before your treatment is complete! Most treatments are _completed within one year,_ however, this can vary from patient to patient.

### Why Choose Clear Aligners?

-   With clear aligners, you can have a _straighter_, _healthier smile_ without all the noticeable wires and brackets.
-   Clear aligners _are removable_ making it easy for you to enjoy the foods you love without worrying about breaking or bending your brackets and wires. You can also remove your clear aligners to brush and floss more easily.
-   With clear aligners, you can continue participating in day-to-day activities with minimal changes to your lifestyle.
-   Office visits and checkups are easy, quick, and painless. Fewer appointments are required throughout your entire treatment.

### How Do Clear Aligners Work?

When you begin your clear aligners treatment you will receive several sets of custom fit aligners. Each set of aligners is worn for 1 – 2 weeks. After that time, you switch your aligners with the next set. Over time you will notice gradual changes in the alignment of your teeth and in just a few months you will have a straighter, healthier smile!

### Spark Aligners: Designed For Clarity & Comfort

Spark Aligners are one of the most advanced clear aligner systems available today. Made with TruGEN™ material, Spark offers:

-   Enhanced stain resistance
-   Greater clarity
-   Increased force retention for more efficient tooth movement

Spark is a favorite among our patients and doctors alike for its aesthetic appeal and treatment performance.

### Invisalign®: A Trusted Name In Clear Aligners

Invisalign remains one of the most recognized brands in orthodontics and is still a treatment option we may recommend based on your case. With decades of research and innovation behind it, Invisalign has helped millions achieve straighter smiles with its SmartTrack® material and customized treatment planning.

O’Gara-Gilbert-Silvaroli Orthodontics uses Invisalign when it aligns best with your orthodontic needs and goals.

**[Schedule a consultation today](/contact/)** to see which clear aligner option is best for your smile!

With a highly trained, professional staff

# Investment + Financing

## The investment of perfecting a smile has a lifetime of return

Investment + Financing[Unravel](https://ogortho.com/author/starburst-pink138/)2026-05-12T21:55:21+00:00

At O’Gara-Gilbert-Silvaroli Orthodontics, we believe everyone deserves a confident, healthy smile. We also believe cost should never be the reason you wait to begin treatment. That’s why we offer flexible payment options, affordable monthly plans, and 0% financing to help make orthodontic care accessible for every family.

During your complimentary consultation, our team will review your treatment needs, insurance benefits, and payment options so you can feel confident moving forward.

## What Is the Cost of Orthodontic Treatment?

The cost of treatment can vary because every smile is unique. Several factors help determine your investment, including:

-   Complexity of the orthodontic issues being corrected
-   Length of treatment
-   Type of treatment recommended
-   Specific treatment plan prescribed by Dr. O’Gara, Dr. Gilbert, or Dr. Silvaroli

Our goal is to make treatment affordable, transparent, and stress-free from the very beginning.

## Flexible Payment Options

We offer a variety of payment options designed to fit your budget, including:

-   Low and no-down payment options
-   0% interest financing
-   Extended monthly payment plans
-   Payment arrangements to coordinate with Flex Plan needs
-   Most major credit cards accepted

Instead of paying one large upfront cost, our financing options allow you to spread payments out over time with manageable monthly installments. Every dollar goes directly toward your care, not added interest.

![](https://ogortho.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/affordable-orthodontic-financing-braces-aligners-og-ortho.jpg "affordable-orthodontic-financing-braces-aligners-og-ortho")

## Personalized Financial Guidance

One thing that truly sets OG Ortho apart is our experienced in-house financial coordinator, who has been helping patients for over 25 years. She will walk you through every step, answer your questions, and help create a plan that works for you.

She can help:

-   Explain payment options clearly
-   Maximize your insurance benefits
-   Create a customized payment plan
-   Coordinate directly with your insurance
-   Make the financial process simple and stress-free

## Insurance

We are a provider for most major insurance plans and are happy to help you understand your orthodontic benefits. During your consultation, we will review your coverage and help determine how your benefits may apply to treatment.

## Why Start Now?

Orthodontic treatment is about more than a beautiful smile. A healthy, properly aligned smile can improve oral hygiene, bite alignment, jaw comfort, chewing function, speech, confidence, and self-esteem.

For children, early treatment may also help prevent more complex issues in the future.

## You Are Your Best Investment

A confident, radiant smile is within reach, and it may be more affordable than you think. Whether you are investing in your child’s smile or finally starting treatment for yourself, OG Ortho is here to make the process simple, supportive, and stress-free.

Schedule your complimentary consultation today by calling [**775-825-1881**](<tel: 775-825-1881>) or visiting [**www.ogortho.com**](http://www.ogortho.com). Let us show you how easy it can be to begin your smile journey.

With a highly trained, professional staff

# Early Treatment

## Timing is everything when it comes to your child’s orthodontic treatment

Early Treatment[Unravel](https://ogortho.com/author/starburst-pink138/)2025-04-02T03:11:21+00:00

### What age should a child visit the orthodontist?

The American Association of Orthodontists recommends that children should have an orthodontic screening by age 7. This allows Dr. O’Gara, Dr. Gilbert, & Dr. Silvaroli to determine if orthodontic treatment will be necessary and the best time for the patient to be treated.

### Why visit an orthodontist by 7?

-   Orthodontists can spot subtle problems with jaw growth and emerging teeth while some baby teeth are still present.
    
-   While your child’s teeth may appear to be straight, there could be a problem that only an orthodontist can detect.
    
-   Early treatment may prevent more serious problems from developing and may make treatment at a later age shorter and less complicated.
    
-   An orthodontist will be able to achieve results that may not be possible once the face and jaws have finished growing.
    

### Why are children being evaluated at an early age?

Early diagnosis and treatment can:

-   Guide erupting teeth into a more favorable position
-   Preserve space for permanent teeth
-   Reduce the likelihood of fracturing protruded front teeth

If early treatment is indicated, we can:

-   Guide the growth of the jaw and incoming permanent teeth
-   Regulate the width of the upper and lower dental arches
-   Avoid the need for permanent tooth extractions
-   Reduce the likelihood of impacted permanent teeth
-   Correct thumb-sucking
-   Improve/eliminate abnormal swallowing
-   Improve/eliminate speech problems

![](https://ogortho.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/04/new-picture-3.jpg "early orthodontic treatment reno image")

### What is an orthodontic observation program?

The orthodontic observation program is for patients who are not ready for treatment yet. This program allows us to monitor the eruptive patterns of the permanent teeth. We see patients on recall every 6-8 months to observe their progress as they grow. This complimentary program allows the orthodontist to determine the optimum time to begin treatment and achieve the best possible result for your child.

### In addition to a beautiful new smile, what are some other benefits of orthodontic treatment?

-   Improve the function of the bite and teeth
    
-   Improve oral hygiene
    
-   Prevent wear on the teeth
    
-   Increase the longevity of natural teeth
    
-   Guide jaw growth
    
-   Lower the risk of trauma to protruded teeth
    
-   Correct harmful oral habit
    
-   Improve appearance and self-esteem
    
-   Guide permanent teeth to a better position
    
-   Improve the way the lips meet
    

### Does early treatment prevent the need for braces as an adolescent?

Early treatment can begin the correction of significant problems, prevent severe issues from developing, and simplify future treatment.

### Do you still need to see your family dentist during orthodontic treatment?

Patients with braces and other orthodontic appliances require more effort to keep their teeth and gums clean. We want to ensure the highest level of dental health, we recommend you see your family dentist for regular check-ups and cleanings every 3-6 months during treatment.

With a highly trained, professional staff

# Adolescent Treatment

## Adolescence is the ideal time for braces

Adolescent Treatment[Unravel](https://ogortho.com/author/starburst-pink138/)2025-04-09T20:56:48+00:00

Between the ages of 11 and 15 is an ideal time for orthodontic treatment. By 12, most permanent teeth have erupted and are in place. Crooked teeth, gaps, and bad bites are more obvious.

It is common for children in this age range to undergo orthodontic treatment.

Contact our office for an initial complimentary examination, and we will be able to determine the best time for treatment and outline the plan and the duration expected.

![adolescent getting braces in Reno, NV](https://ogortho.com/wp-content/uploads/2022/07/AdobeStock_292296207-min-1.jpeg "AdobeStock_292296207-min")

With a highly trained, professional staff

# Adult Treatment

## There is no age limit on having a healthy, confident smile

Adult Treatment[Unravel](https://ogortho.com/author/starburst-pink138/)2024-02-29T21:48:16+00:00

Orthodontics is not just for children and teens. In fact, 1 in every 5 patients in orthodontic treatment is an adult. No one is too old to attain a beautiful smile.

Some adults had orthodontic problems as children but were unable to correct them until now. Others, who had treatment as children, may need further treatment as adults due to relapse or limitations in initial treatment.

Starting treatment is easy! O’Gara-Gilbert-Silvaroli Orthodontics offers a complimentary initial exam and consultation including records, photos, x-rays, and treatment plan.

Dr. O’Gara, Dr. Gilbert, and Dr. Silvaroli have treated individuals in their 50’s and beyond!

### Benefits of Treatment

**Adults can benefit from orthodontic treatment in many ways.**

-   Align the teeth to create a more aesthetic and beautiful smile
    
-   Correct relapse after previous orthodontic treatment due to lack of long term retainer wear
    
-   Improve the ability to brush and floss, especially for crowded or overlapping lower front teeth
    
-   Establish a more comfortable bite and ability to chew
    
-   Address missing teeth
    
-   Close spaces between the teeth
    
-   Reduce excessive wear on the teeth due to grinding or poor bite
    
-   Jaw surgery to correct a discrepancy between the upper and lower jaws.
    

![](https://ogortho.com/wp-content/uploads/2022/07/AdobeStock_322857625-min-1.jpeg "AdobeStock_322857625-min")

### Appearance of Braces

Many adults are concerned with the appearance of braces. Often, Invisalign® (The invisible alternative to braces) can be used to treat adults. This technology involves clear removable aligners, fabricated through advanced 3D imaging to align the teeth. For more severe problems, we have the option of clear ceramic brackets instead of metal.

### Soreness/Discomfort

Typically, the teeth are most uncomfortable 12-24 hours after an orthodontic adjustment and will disappear within 3 to 5 days. If necessary, over-the-counter pain medications can ease your discomfort. Updated appliances are smaller and more comfortable to wear. Modern techniques, with the new titanium technology, put less pressure on the teeth while moving the teeth more rapidly and efficiently, decreasing the time required for adjustments to every 8 – 10 weeks.

### Investment

The investment for adult orthodontic treatment is determined by your customized treatment plan. It is based on the severity of the problem, the complexity of the correction, the appliances used, and the length of time to correct.

With a highly trained, professional staff

# Retainers

## Wear them!

Retainers[Unravel](https://ogortho.com/author/starburst-pink138/)2024-02-29T21:51:23+00:00

### Removable Retainers

Once the appliances are removed, you will receive retainers to stabilize your teeth. The bone and soft tissues surrounding the teeth are stabilizing for several months after braces are removed. It is **IMPERATIVE** that the retainers are worn as instructed. Failure to wear the retainers will result in undesirable movement of the teeth, which will necessitate re-treatment.

### How Retainers are Worn

Teeth will continue to move throughout life. Individuals who have undergone orthodontic therapy must wear retainers indefinitely. Full-time wear is prescribed for the first year.

After one year, the patient can wear the retainer just at night. OG Ortho will see you routinely post-treatment.

### How to Care for Retainers

-   Retainers are made of a thermoplastic material that is sensitive to excessive heat, so they should be kept away from heaters, dishwashers, car dashboards or any place where the temperatures exceed 115 degrees (Fº).
-   Retainers should be kept in the proper retainer case when they are not in the patient’s mouth.
-   Keep away from any family pets, as they like to chew on them.
-   Clean before and after placement in the mouth. To clean your retainers, DO NOT use toothpaste, it is abrasive and will damage your retainers. Instead, brush your retainers with cool water before and after you wear them. If a more thorough cleanse is necessary, soak them in a 50/50 mix of hydrogen peroxide and water for up to 15 minutes.

Retainers are worn for years and therefore will need periodic replacement. Your original treatment contract includes one set of retainers after the braces are removed. If the retainers are lost or damaged due to neglect, there will be a charge for replacement. If anything becomes loose or broken, contact our office immediately.

![](https://ogortho.com/wp-content/uploads/2022/07/removable-teeth-aligners-on-the-blue-background-wi-2022-01-28-10-46-30-utc.jpg "Removable teeth aligners on the blue background with copy space. Orthodontic treatment for beautiful smile.")

### Fixed Retainers (Bonded Wire)

The fixed wire will protect your teeth. Please have this wire monitored by your family dentist at your six-month checkups.

### Habits or Conditions that Require Stronger Retention

-   Mouth breathing
-   Clenching and/or grinding of teeth (muscle hyperactivity)
-   Tongue thrusting, low tongue posture or abnormal tongue posture
-   Playing a musical instruments with a reed
-   Lip sucking
-   Pen or pencil biting
-   Spacing between the teeth prior to treatment
-   Severely rotated teeth
-   Nail biting

Our goal is to help your smile stay beautiful and healthy for a lifetime. Your cooperation is essential in order to achieve this goal.

### Dental Examinations and Cleanings

The patient should continue with proper oral hygiene procedures at home, including thorough brushing and flossing techniques. Family dentist should be seen every 3 – 6 months for cleaning and dental examinations.

With a highly trained, professional staff

# i-CAT® 3D Imaging

## Quick, comfortable, and effective dental imaging for the most precise dental health diagnosis possible

i-CAT® 3D Imaging[Unravel](https://ogortho.com/author/starburst-pink138/)2022-07-21T21:32:27+00:00

### We offer i-CAT® 3D imaging

We love our iCAT 3-D Dental Imaging System. It uses 95% less radiation than traditional hospital-based CT scan machines and gives us a 3-dimensional image of the patient’s teeth and jaws. This allows our doctors to precisely plan the exact movement of the teeth without the guesswork of traditional or digital X-rays, which are only 2-dimensional.

In addition to moving teeth, braces affect the position and shape of the surrounding bone, gums, lips, cheeks and muscles of the face. With our 3-D CT scan, we now have the ability to create a complete treatment plan. This gives our patients a more beautiful and healthy smile in an extremely precise manner.

The iCAT 3-D Dental Imaging System provides un-paralleled technology that produces optimal images. As patients increasingly ask for more sophisticated and immediate diagnostic results, our advanced in-office imaging system provides our doctors with more accurate information for diagnosis and treatment planning. This includes a wide range of orthodontic problems such as TMJ disorders, enlarged tonsils or adenoids, impacted or missing permanent teeth and other dental abnormalities. Anatomy we once guessed at is now visible through our iCAT system.

![i-CAT imaging offered by our Reno orthodontists](https://ogortho.com/wp-content/uploads/2022/07/i-CAT_DEXIS_Product_Header_0_0.png "i-CAT_DEXIS_Product_Header_0_0")

With a highly trained, professional staff

# Opalescence Teeth Whitening

## Safe, Gentle, & Long-Lasting Professional Teeth Whitening

Opalescence Teeth Whitening[Unravel](https://ogortho.com/author/starburst-pink138/)2025-09-22T21:10:20+00:00

O’Gara-Gilbert-Silvaroli Orthodontics is proud to offer Opalescence™ Home Advanced, professional-strength whitening trays designed to give you a brighter smile safely and effectively. Unlike over-the-counter kits, Opalescence is dentist-recommended, long-lasting, and customized for real results.

## Why Choose Opalescence Whitening?

-   **Fast & Effective**: enjoy visible results quickly
-   **Safe & Gentle**: formulated to protect your enamel and gums
-   **Long-Lasting:** a brighter smile that outlasts store-bought strips
-   **Non-Invasive**: no drills, no discomfort, just a whiter smile

## Affordable Whitening, Professional Results

Get 10 treatments for just $89.99, available directly in our office. Safe, convenient, and backed by dental-grade quality, Opalescence makes achieving a brighter smile easier than ever.

![](https://ogortho.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/Opalescence-Teeth-Whitening-image.jpg "Opalescence Teeth Whitening image")

## Why Patients Trust OG Ortho

Our orthodontic team is committed to more than just straight teeth; we want you to love your smile. From braces and clear aligners to advanced whitening solutions, we provide expert care with treatments designed to fit your lifestyle. Families throughout Reno, Sparks, and Northern Nevada trust OG Ortho for orthodontic care they can count on.

[Visit Our Office](https://ogortho.com/contact/)

### FAQs

#### [Is Opalescence safe?](#b0446f457eafb8883)

Yes. Opalescence is dentist-recommended and formulated to protect your teeth and gums while whitening.

#### [Will Opalescence make my teeth sensitive?](#52f0218df0a9bf4dc)

Some people may notice temporary sensitivity, but most patients find the treatment very comfortable compared to over-the-counter kits.

#### [How long do whitening results last?](#acc644e9b557c615b)

With good oral hygiene and limited staining habits (like coffee, tea, or smoking), your results can last for months. Touch-ups are easy and affordable.

#### [How is Opalescence different from store-bought whitening strips?](#83c8d6cd344e2b6f9)

Opalescence uses professional-strength ingredients and custom application trays, delivering more consistent and longer-lasting results.

With a highly trained, professional staff

# Before & After

## Real Smiles. Real Results.

Before & After[Unravel](https://ogortho.com/author/starburst-pink138/)2026-03-09T17:25:05+00:00

## See the OG Ortho Difference

Every smile has a story — and we love being part of it. Browse through some of our patients’ incredible transformations and see firsthand what personalized, expert orthodontic care at O’Gara-Gilbert-Silvaroli Orthodontics can do.

[![](https://ogortho.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/before-after-2026.03_01.jpg "before after 2026.03_01")](https://ogortho.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/before-after-2026.03_01.jpg)

A confident smile is built step by step. With a personalized treatment plan, advanced technology such as i-CAT® 3D imaging, and a team dedicated to your success, achieving the smile you’ve always wanted is closer than you think.

We welcome patients of all ages—children, teens, and adults—and look forward to helping you discover what’s possible for your smile.

# Troubleshooting Your Braces

## What to do for mishaps?

Troubleshooting Your Braces[Unravel](https://ogortho.com/author/starburst-pink138/)2025-04-02T03:12:45+00:00

Occasionally, some patients will experience potential complications during treatment.

### Issues Related to Orthodontic Appliances

Appliances might become broken or bent during your orthodontic care, such as loose bands, loose brackets, or broken or poking wires. For your convenience and the convenience of scheduled patients, it is not possible to handle complications on a walk-in basis.  Call our office for an appointment during patient hours.

In the meantime… here are some helpful hints ↓

-   If a wire is irritating your mouth, cover it with beeswax or tuck it under the archwire with the end of a pencil eraser, or cut with nail clippers.
    
-   A loose band or bracket can generally be left in place until seen in our office. Call us to schedule a repair appointment.
    
-   If a [severe injury](https://ogortho.com/orthodontic-emergencies/) occurs seek immediate medical care.
    

![](https://ogortho.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/04/new-picture-4.jpg "braces in reno image 2")

### Direct Injuries to the Mouth and Teeth

Following a direct injury, immediately ice, and contact your regular dentist and our office as soon as possible. If a tooth has been displaced, knocked out, or fractured, contact your family dentist first and then notify our practice.

### Common Orthodontic Issues

As much as we try to avoid orthodontic issues, they do occasionally arise. If you experience any of the below situations, please contact us.

#### [Tooth Discomfort and Soreness](#7be573c7f98a3d20a)

The discomfort that can arise as your teeth move to their correct positions will feel more like discomfort or soreness than actual pain.  This is normal and nothing to worry about! To mitigate any discomfort take over-the-counter pain relievers to manage the discomfort.

As your teeth move into their correct positions, they may feel loose – don’t worry. They are merely shifting correctly. The looseness will fade as you get accustomed to the movement.

If your discomfort does not subside or feels more like a shooting sensation, there could be an issue. Call our office if you’re worried about pain.

#### [Loose or Broken Brackets](#466f6803c5edd922f)

Loose brackets or brackets detached from your teeth can happen. This can result from eating hard/sticky foods, playing with the brackets, bumping into something with your braces, or from a multitude of other reasons.

This is not a serious emergency and does not impose on the effectiveness of your braces.

If you have a loose bracket or if a bracket breaks, call our office to fix it. In the meantime, leave it in place and cover it with wax if needed. If the bracket breaks off entirely, you can carefully remove it and we will repair it during your next appointment.

#### [Ulcers or Sores Inside Your Mouth](#5c9d7f52bf93d81b4)

As your mouth gets used to having braces on your teeth, it is normal for the braces to cause irritation and small sores or ulcers in your mouth.

While they can be uncomfortable, these irritations are normal. Here are some ways you can help ease any discomfort:

-   Avoid touching them with your fingers.
-   Cover the irritating bracket or wire with wax and/or take over-the-counter pain medications.
-   You may also use oral topical gels.

These issues are normal and generally go away in time. However, if sores and ulcers continue to arise and are causing significant discomfort, call our office.

#### [Wire Slipped Out Completely](#919da0c70813a5d92)

The wire on your braces can slip out completely. If this happens, call our office.

Try to fit it back into the molar tube with your fingers or with clean tweezers. If you can’t fit it back in, you can also clip it back with clean nail clippers and remove the loose piece.

#### [Poking Steel Tie](#8809ce2bfdfc331a1)

If your braces use steel ligatures to connect wires and brackets, these sometimes can unwind and poke your lips, gums, or other parts of your mouth. If this happens, call our office.

Try to push it back into place with your finger, clean eraser end of a pencil, or use wax to cover the end of the poking wire.

#### [Lost Rubber Band](#1470f3bfb14f5fa80)

We place elastic bands over the brackets to help secure the archwire on your braces. If one of these bands comes off, it’s okay.

If this happens, you can try to place the rubber band back on with your fingers or clean tweezers, or leave it off until your next appointment. If the wire starts getting loose or the bracket starts to irritate your mouth, call our office.

### After Office Hours

If an orthodontic emergency arises where the patient is in severe and/or unusual pain after hours, please contact our office. Information is available regarding who to contact for further assistance: [(775) 825-1881](tel:17758251881).

With a highly trained, professional staff

# Orthodontic Emergencies

## If you experience an emergency with your braces, call our office for help!

Orthodontic Emergencies[Unravel](https://ogortho.com/author/starburst-pink138/)2025-04-02T03:09:22+00:00

### Orthodontic Emergencies

Orthodontic emergencies are situations that require immediate attention from your orthodontist. They can include things like a broken appliance, or severe pain. If you have an orthodontic emergency, please contact our staff right away. We will help prevent further damage and resolve any issues you may be experiencing.

[CALL US: (775) 825-1881](tel:17758251881)

![](https://ogortho.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/04/new-picture-2-1.jpg "orthodontic emergencies image")

### Types Of Orthodontic Emergencies

As much as we try to avoid orthodontic emergencies, they do occasionally arise. If you experience any of the below situations, please contact us to help.

#### [Wire Poking Cheeks or Gums](#61933cfd0de5d8008)

If the ends of your archwires are poking out and causing discomfort; there are several things you can do to help:

-   Push the wire back into the tube using the disinfected eraser end of a pencil or a clean cotton swab.
-   Place a ball of wax on the end of the wire to protect your gums.

If the wire needs to be cut, use clean disinfected nail clippers. Use rubbing alcohol to clean the clippers, and then trim the wire back to a comfortable length. You may need a mirror or a family member to assist you.

#### [Trauma or Injury](#53c9f012942a74252)

Trauma or injuries to your face are true orthodontic emergencies and require immediate treatment. If you experience trauma or an injury to your face, seek immediate medical attention. Once you are cleared, call our office to help resolve any resulting orthodontic issues.

#### [Infection](#977a8c2e2a46eec9c)

If you have bleeding or sore gums, tooth, gum or jaw pain, you may have an oral infection. Oral infections can become serious if not treated immediately. If you suspect you may have an infection, call our office immediately.

#### [Swelling or Bleeding](#1e73d1633f0be310d)

Swelling or bleeding in your gums or mouth can be signs of more serious issues. If you are experiencing painful swelling or bleeding during your orthodontic treatment, call our office.

With a highly trained, professional staff

# First Visit

## Discover orthodontics solutions to fit your desired aesthetic and your lifestyle

First Visit[Unravel](https://ogortho.com/author/starburst-pink138/)2024-02-29T21:53:06+00:00

### What to Expect

Upon your arrival at O’Gara-Gilbert-Silvaroli Orthodontics, our friendly receptionists will greet you and notify the doctors that you have arrived. During your appointment, Dr. William O’Gara, Dr. Gilbert, or Dr. Silvaroli will conduct a thorough clinical orthodontic examination and explain all aspects of treatment.

### Initial exam will answer:

-   What orthodontic problems are present, if any?
-   When is the proper time to begin treatment?
-   What type of treatment is recommended?
-   How long will treatment take?
-   How much will the treatment cost?

### What’s next?

If orthodontic treatment is recommended at this time, an appointment for Diagnostic Records is scheduled. If time allows, these diagnostic records can be taken at the initial visit.

![](https://ogortho.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/10/work-of-modern-dentist-orthodontist-with-children-EM3YAQT.jpg "work-of-modern-dentist-orthodontist-with-children-EM3YAQT")

### What are diagnostic records?

-   Study models of your teeth
-   Panoramic and cephalogram radiographs and tracings
-   Doctor’s studying diagnostic records and determining the best course of treatment

### What is the cost of treatment?

The cost of treatment depends upon the severity of the patient’s problem. Your treatment coordinator will discuss fees and payment options during the initial exam. We offer various payment plans to suit your budget and accept assignment from most insurance plans.

We work hard to make orthodontics affordable.

### What does my investment include?

Your investment includes:

-   Orthodontic records, which consist of x-rays, study models, and a personalized growth study workup to determine your treatment objectives.
-   Active treatment fee for both early treatment options and complete comprehensive treatment. Treatment via braces or Invisalign includes all appliances, office visits, and procedures performed in the office. Appliances are durable and should last through the entire treatment period if the patient follows instructions. If appliances require replacement due to loss, breakage, misuse, or failure to eliminate foods that cause damage, we reserve the right to charge an additional replacement fee.
-   One set of final retainers for our comprehensive (Phase II) patients is provided upon completion of treatment. There is a replacement fee for each additional retainer that is lost or broken.

### What doesn’t my investment include?

-   Work performed at other dental offices outside our orthodontic practice.
-   If an accident or injury occurs during treatment which results in TMJ (Temporo-Mandibular-Joint) complications, additional orthodontics, orthognathic surgery, etc, a fee adjustment may be required to complete treatment under these unusual circumstances.

### Assignment of Benefits

We accept the assignment of benefits for most insurance plans. By doing this, the family’s out-of-pocket expense is substantially lower. If we do not accept the assignment of benefits from your plan, we will be happy to file claims so that the insured can be reimbursed directly. Should there be any change in your policy prior to full payment of the insurance benefit, please [contact](/contact/) our office immediately so adjustments may be made to your contract.

### Office Hours

It is our goal to see you, on time, for your appointment. Time is scheduled to complete the necessary procedure, give information about the treatment, and answer any questions you may have. In order for us to maintain a schedule that runs in a timely manner, we request that patients arrive on time to avoid delays, as the day progresses. Your cooperation and consideration with appointments are greatly appreciated by our office and the patients that are seen after your appointment.

With a highly trained, professional staff

## Thank you for voting O’Gara-Gilbert-Silvaroli Orthodontics as Best Orthodontist in 2026!

We’re thrilled to announce that O’Gara-Gilbert-Silvaroli Orthodontics has been named Best Orthodontist in the 2026 Best of Reno Community’s Choice Awards! This incredible honor is a testament to the trust and support of our amazing patients and the Reno community who voted to recognize the exceptional care we strive to deliver every day.

The Best of Reno Awards celebrate the top businesses and organizations across more than 180 categories, and being selected as a winner is a true community-driven achievement. Thank you for making us your favorite. We’re proud to serve you and honored to be part of what makes Reno shine!
